	focus = {
		id = CHI_the_second_thrid_congress_of_the_kmt
		icon = GFX_focus_generic_china1
		prerequisite = { focus = CHI_hoist_the_red_flag focus = CHI_empower_party_democracy }
		mutually_exclusive = { }
		x = 1
		y = 1
		cost = 10
		relative_position_id = CHI_hoist_the_red_flag

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1
		}

		available = {
			is_subject = no
			OR = {
				has_government = communism
				has_government = democratic
			}
		}

		bypass = {

		}

		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		continue_if_invalid = no
		available_if_capitulated = no

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }
		completion_reward = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = CHI_empower_party_democracy }
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = communism
					elections_allowed = yes
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = democratic_socialism
			}
			add_stability = 0.1
			add_war_support = 0.15
			add_political_power = 100
			IF = {
				limit = {
					KCG = { 
						exists = yes
						NOT = { original_tag= SWK}
					}
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = CHI
				}
				add_claim_by = ROOT
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					KCG = { 
						exists = yes 
						NOT = { original_tag = SWK }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_democratic_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@democratic }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_democratic_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_communism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@communism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_communism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_neutrality_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_neutrality_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_fascism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@fascism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_fascism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = democratic
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = communism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = fascism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_wargoal_against = ROOT
					}
					remove_wargoal = {
					    type = all
				 	   target = ROOT
					}
				}
				KCG = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = ROOT
						autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_popularities = { #Denmark keeps it government form
						democratic = ROOT_democratic_popularity
						fascism = ROOT_fascism_popularity
						communism = ROOT_communism_popularity
						neutrality = ROOT_neutrality_popularity
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
							}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = communism }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
					}
					
				}
			}

		}
	}
